## Concerns and limits

1. **Qualification stopped at dag_peer_inventory.** The bounded record deliberately gives only the family ID, not a raw exception or subcase. Static inspection finds a concrete self-shadowing hazard: its first text.replace for B's needs string can match the same literal in the earlier controls heredoc, rather than the actual B needs line. validateWorkflow then sees the unchanged DAG and the rejection assertion can fail. This is a static inference consistent with the failure, not a separately executed pinpoint reproduction. The precise first failed subcase was not logged. It was not fixed or rerun in this unit.
2. **Emitted verifier-record semantics deviate from SPEC.** The receiver successfully validated a strict current pair and emitted status verified / proof strict_current_safe_pair, but then changed reason to invalid_evidence when the controls producer was failed. A valid nonpass should retain reason none and accepted false; malformed evidence is separate. Root independently identified this deviation. The exact emitted record is preserved above; it is not presented as a clean receiver result.
3. **Five wrapper controls and inline split syntax inspection are unexecuted.** Key-scope, fixed cases, metadata/custody/AEAD, state-table and complete current-pair wrapper qualification therefore remain unproven, despite their preserved source and static inspection. Actual Native preparation/consumption is unexecuted. Passing 16 ZIP controls does not fill these gaps.
4. Fresh independent task/spec/quality review is required after closure. Any correction or another qualification requires a new concrete Root release. Task 2 remains HELD; no source fix, activation, retry, main merge or runtime action follows this report automatically.

## Finding-to-fix mapping and covering seams

All seven Important findings were implemented and covered by this qualification. None was adjudicated away or parked. Their final disposition belongs to the original independent reviewer's scoped re-review.

| Finding | Concrete source change | Executed covering evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| F1: mutation self-shadowing | Actual job headers are selected by anchored names; actual steps by unique fixed IDs. Every mutation requires exactly one target, proves the target changed and proves the surrounding Source bytes remained unchanged. Validator helper bodies and hashes cannot substitute for the behavioral target. | Actual B needs and attempt endpoint, actual environment/key step identities, readonly TAP count, B cases and every F8 upload property reject their targeted bad edits. |
| F2: early pure receipt | New minimal shared finalizePureBinding invokes the unchanged original keyless World verification/profile selection/materialization closure, then exclusively writes the fixed receipt. All six actual pure_binding steps use it. Public projection also requires captured pure_binding success. | Real bindPure succeeds on inert input; rejecting inert original-stage closure produces zero writes; successful closure produces exactly one fixed exclusive0600 write. A leftover valid receipt with failed pure step projects pure=null, and observed Native after failed pure is rejected. |
| F3: observation/peak/ownership | Original private coherent-proof adds only authenticated observedAt. B captures Date.now once with its successful final inventory, uses that observation for the sweep and stores it. Reopen recomputes peak from the authenticated inventory and exact observedAt, requires captured equality and projects the recomputed peak. Own start<=observation<=sealedAt and every observed peer end<=observation are checked. Actual ownership order is positive_producer,reduction_producer,R1,R2,R3. | Positive private proof accepted; contradictory captured peak2 against computed1 rejected; early/late observation, future peer endings, wrong ownership identities, altered protected clock deltas, overlap and graph inventory rejected. Native-only peak3/4 and tie ordering tested with inert job metadata. |
| F4: final inventory/nonpass reconciliation | B retains its separate early seven-plus-optional-nonexecuting-receiver rule. Final verifySeven requires exact8 unique jobs with verify in_progress and seven legacy identities. Both Jobs call sites use current-run attempts/1/jobs. New shared reconcileSuite inspects every complete available direct needs assignment even when public proof is null and validates current by-ID metadata without private downloads. | Complete passed route accepted; absent/queued receiver and nonterminal legacy observations produce upstream unverified; terminal failed B produces not_all_seven_success. Nonpass fixture proves all7 available assignments were checked, including5 peers. Missing outputs are never fabricated. Source/run/head/ref/direct-output mismatches reject acceptance. |
| F5: pair validity vs producer result | Once strict transport/schema is verified, status verified / reason none / strict proof is retained for valid nonpass or unverified upstream. accepted/upstream carry rejection. Receiver-control failure after trusted bootstrap returns the same closed <=4096B unavailable/invalid_evidence/absent record, retaining known SAFE ID/digest and no ZIP claims. | Real route+reconciliation on a valid failed controls pair returns verified/none/strict/acceptedfalse. A direct self-control failure exercises the actual guard, checks retained assignment, null ZIP claim, exact closed failure semantics and byte bound. Actual published passed pair returns acceptedtrue. |
| F6: untested real decisions | Added adapter projectSuiteOutcome over exact six captured outcomes and validated receipts; actual B publisher calls it. Added verifier reconcileSuite; actual verify calls it with fixed IO. Real validators bind each of26 key steps to its exact job/step, check actual always guards/order/final truth tests, the complete original F8 action block and pure gate wiring. Fixed named subcase phase diagnostics localize failures without public schema changes. | Same publication/route/reconciliation seams exercised with native/aggregate/metadata/open/inner/skipped/cancelled outcomes, retained assignments, all actual26 key scopes, full F8 mutations, publication guard edits, final metadata and schema contradictions. 18 named subcases each logged start+passed. |
| F7: operation cap/timeout drift | Fixed trusted operation enum: b-peer raw134283264/20000ms; generic-private raw136314880/30000ms; pure134283264/30000ms; SMALLSAFE131072/30000ms. Metadata GET timeout remains10000ms. assignedPrivate own/producer uses generic-private; B's5 peers and raw pure use b-peer. Shared checkedDownloadSize is used by the actual bounded stream collector. | Exact metadata cap accepted/cap+1 rejected for every domain; shared size accumulator accepts exact edge/rejects edge+1 without large allocation; inert streams exercise real reader; injected internal timeout capture proves20000 vs30000. Arbitrary operation rejected. |

M1's elected improvement is implemented within F1/F6: fixed subcase IDs emit only family, ID, phase and false flags. No exception, raw value or private path is emitted. The six-ID public controls DTO remains unchanged.

The fixed internal projection API is:

```text
projectSuiteOutcome({
  outcomes:{pureBinding,native,aggregate,ownMetadata,ownOpen,ownValidation},
  receipts:{pure,assignment,proof}
}) -> existing exact SuiteResult
```

Each outcome is success|failure|skipped|cancelled|not_observed. Actual wrapper env bindings read only the six fixed GHA step IDs. Assignment is preserved even after metadata/open/inner failure. A failed pure step cannot confer verification from a leftover file; contradictory observed Native is rejected. Native-step failure remains distinct from the legacy native+aggregate composite used by the original private manifest.

The split aggregate entry now uses scoped always()+!cancelled()+trusted bootstrap+native success, then checks every fixed peer collector/open prerequisite before any World import or aggregate read. A failed prerequisite yields an entered aggregate failure/evidence_unverified; skipped aggregate remains not_observed. It does not fabricate an outcome or call World on missing inputs.

All actual entry points use fixed IO and fixed Source closures. Test capture IO/inert callbacks are internal control seams only; there is no CLI/env path, transport, cap, timeout or fixture override in the actual entry. The unchanged original business oracle remains the real World path; the shared helpers own orchestration, projection, custody and reconciliation decisions.

## Preserved business pipeline and scope

The six original Native/TAP command blocks were compared as complete Source strings and remain byte-for-byte exact. The full original F8 sealer-through-upload block is also exact:19458 bytes/SHA256 `8b239b4a5e7e4d5f424fef645a464e006c039a47763224902c4221bba2666231`; the protected World/F8 literal itself remains unchanged under its original blob pin. F8 controls now check the entire actual block and mutate the real upload action version/name/path/retention/error behavior.

Original seven-job DAG, timeouts, six environments, protected tests/assertions/World, R120s/P-D300s, root30s/child5s, C0=I0+900000 and C1=C0+180000 remain unchanged. No A/positive dependency was added to B. Legacy B composite COMMAND_OK remains Native success AND aggregate success; public command still observes the full original Native+TAP step separately.

All eight actual candidate jobs remain literally false; inactive candidate is manual/no-input/no-push. Existing actual key is confined to26 expected generic crypto steps, with zero job-level key bindings. Pure binding, World semantics, metadata/raw reads, installation, manifest/copy preparation, projection and verifier remain keyless. The original five-lineage/ten-graph/ownership/all-D/overlap/deadline business proof is not replaced with a typed case oracle.

One new private-only observedAt field was authorized; no public timestamp or DTO field was added. The exact original/reopened private proof bytes, captured hash and authenticated reopen path bind that observation. No self-artifact ID is inserted into the private proof.

Generic custody limits and protected modules are unchanged. The operation-specific reader restores the generic-private130MiB raw domain while preserving B's tighter original raw cap and20-second timeout. Pure per-JSON2097152, ZIP3 expanded134225920 and SMALLSAFE combined65536 remain separate.

These are implementation and covering-test claims, not this writer's re-review disposition. The original reviewer must assess the scoped delta before any actual suite release.

The new production loading API returns only the existing internal receipt object:

```text
loadProjectionReceipts({outcomes,assignment}, io=fixedProjectionIO)
  -> {pure,assignment,proof}
```

It validates the existing exact six outcome fields, requires keyless scope and uses only fixed runner-private pure-assignment.json and suite-proof.json paths in actual IO. No CLI/env/transport/path override is added. It accepts typed internal receipt bytes; fatal UTF8 decoding and JSON parsing occur only after eligibility. A parse failure after a claimed successful eligible write remains unexpected invalid evidence; it does not fabricate a command outcome or relax the DTO.

Actual B publisher now captures the six outcomes and complete upload assignment first, calls this loader, then calls the existing shared projectSuiteOutcome and publishPair. It no longer eagerly parses optional receipt files. Assignment is independent of either file read.

Suite final reconciliation can update upstream from actual job/assignment observations even when pair transport/schema is unavailable. It preserves unavailable/publication_unavailable or invalid_evidence/absent and known SAFE ID/digest; accepted staysfalse. It does not request SAFE metadata/ZIP for absent outputs, does not request SAFE ZIP for partial outputs, does not invent missing direct assignments and never decrypts peer artifacts. Current controls-kind routing remains separate in the ten-job graph.

## Historical correction: whole-job totals are not per-copy limits

The earlier characterization of a normal successful WHOLE job envelope as at most512 files/128MiB was wrong. The protected World1..512-files/128MiB checks apply to one record.persist snapshot/copy. Generic custody computes its receipt across the complete sourceDir, which includes multiple outputs/copies and ancillary manifests, programs and logs. Its original whole-package maximum is4096 files/512MiB plain bytes.

This wave applies those original generic totals to the outer receipt: successful package fileCount>=1, partial/failure package fileCount>=0, both <=4096 and totalBytes<=512MiB. The original World per-copy limit, generic custody module, independent ZIP/envelope/download/JSON limits and all clocks remain unchanged. Prior statements and test records are preserved above, not edited retrospectively. The earlier aggregate513-reject control is replaced by generic4097-reject and positive multiple-copy aggregation coverage.

This correction does **not** identify the cause of the historical actual custody failures. No private counts, error values or producer logs were read. The reviewer noted that this outer check was not reached in readonly seal. Actual suite43414661211/1 remains failed and unqualified; no provider, credential or runtime cause is inferred from the new source correction.

### Compatibility finding and trusted-startup boundary

The prior failure marker established green_tests failures d12/d13, without raw assertion values. Subsequent official Node22 source confirms a compatibility deficit: bootstrap installs a data-valued default prepareStackTrace function, while the prior blanket guard rejected any such property. This Source finding is separate from unobserved cloud assertion details and does not qualify a repair. References: [Node22.23.4 realm bootstrap](https://github.com/nodejs/node/blob/v22.23.4/lib/internal/bootstrap/realm.js), [Node22.23.4 error formatting](https://github.com/nodejs/node/blob/v22.23.4/lib/internal/errors.js). The default formatting path can read name,code,message on Node-branded errors, so inherited code descriptors are included in the guard/counter controls.

Root/Astra ruled that a function name, source text, lookalike closure or late-catch snapshot cannot authenticate the initial formatter. Fix1 instead captures the actual default descriptor/reference synchronously in the FIRST trusted World dependency, before thirdparty Source dependency evaluation. Only trusted pinned SYN code under controlled Node22.23.4 startup is covered. Arbitrary code substitution before that first capture is OUTSIDE the guarantee. This is not generic compromised-process security; no formatter is deleted, overwritten or temporarily disabled.

Runtime startup fails closed unless Node22.23.4 is exact, NODE_OPTIONS is absent/empty, and process execArgv contains only the explicitly admitted non-preload flags --experimental-vm-modules,--test,--test-reporter=tap,--input-type=module. Qualification is stricter at its entry: both shell roles require NODE_OPTIONS unset before any Node bootstrap, and Q requires execArgv empty. The stdin-module flag is used only by fixed pinned fresh-child test input; it is not an authorization to execute arbitrary input. Preload/reflection fixtures below do not claim protection against arbitrary code that already ran before capture.

### Early runtime and late pure projector

Runtime imports ONLY node:util,node:assert/strict,node:process. No filesystem/network/key/World/SDK/Native dependency or await. It captures trusted intrinsic reflection/type primitives, global Error DATA descriptor/reference, Error/AssertionError prototype descriptors, actual default prepareStackTrace data function/reference+attributes along constructor chain, stackTraceLimit descriptor and the native stack getter/setter descriptor. Capture refuses accessor/proxy/unadmitted baseline shapes without invoking them. Captured state remains lexical/private; runtime exports only readNativeStack.

Before any native getter call, runtime rechecks startup assumptions; exact current global Error descriptor/ref; current original prototype property/parent chains; exact captured default formatter descriptor/ref/attributes at each constructor-chain object; exact name/message/code/toString/constructor/Symbol.toStringTag descriptors on admitted original prototypes; primitive bounded own name/message/code data and absence of own formatting/constructor/tag hooks; exact native stack getter/setter descriptor. No name/text whitelist or unconditional function acceptance. Refusal throws only fixed null sentinel. Native raw stack text stays internal between reader and projector, never output/serialized by this role. No globals are mutated by runtime/projector.

Late helper imports only node:util and contains ZERO Error/AssertionError/global formatter snapshot/new Error reads at import. Accessor stacks require OPTIONAL second argument readNativeStack from the trusted lexical caller; absence or proxy callback refuses as unknown. Error input cannot supply that function. Data stacks and fixed canonical log DTO parsing retain their old contract, bounds and fourteen exact code enums. Both public projections return only fresh {code,site} fixed vocabulary. No message, stack, arbitrary number/path/column/property/error serialization is public.
